# Merge deletion list (farming absorb, Phase 11d unit 5)

## What this file is

The written, rename-aware record of every name that was present on a parent of the
farming absorb merge and is deliberately absent from the merged tree, plus the names the
absorb phases authored that exist on no parent. It is the human half of the export
and symbol census; `scripts/merge_audit/symbol_census.mjs` is the machine half and reads
BOTH kinds of table below. The census asserts, for every exported symbol, content-table
row id, i18n catalog key, and SimEvent name present on ANY parent (ours UNION theirs
UNION the synced release tips), that the name is present in the merged tree unless a
deletion row here says otherwise, and that every name present on NO parent is explained.
The explained set is the script's `EXPLAINED_EXTRAS` constant UNION every row of every
table under an `## Explained extras` heading in this file (`parseExplainedExtras`;
CONSUMED since Phase 17, no longer a mere mirror, so a new phase's extras are appended
here as a dated section rather than grown into the constant). A defective extras row
(missing name, phase, or ruling, or a reason saying only "deleted") fails the census
exactly like a defective deletion row.

The refs (immutable; the census reads every parent with `git ls-tree` plus
`git cat-file --batch`, never a checkout):

| Ref | Commit | Role |
|---|---|---|
| base | `e56707a675013fc1a86bb19d31a0a8d79a02a197` | merge base (informational: the census compares the parent union against merged, never base; base presence drives the release-attribution annotation) |
| ours | `d5304a78c4a1add6b1ed5a0b66ddb9f8246a4d73` | pre-merge `feature/masterwrought` tip, first parent of the absorb merge `424ce89a20` |
| theirs | `8cd964d599ebbb6800fc20741690a0b9b6f17b40` | `origin/feature/farming-plan`, second parent of the absorb merge |
| release | `f50b30de296945379f8d6076ecc56cca617b8e49` | the third parent: the release/v0.40.0 tip synced at Phase 11d STEP 0 (PR #3549); later sync merges contribute their second parents automatically, and `--release` overrides the tip |
| release (2) | `35a64818251d4eeaf0ee849b9e05db57634463a4` | the FOURTH parent: the release/v0.40.0 tip synced at Phase 11d QA STEP 0 (PR #3506), merged at `5eade5c02e`. Auto-derived off the first-parent merge chain, not configured; the census reports "release parent(s) (2)" and the run needs no flag |
| prior sync tip | `65b91fa190f1b6d7935ac38774b1742c3e93bc9c` | the release tip the branch had already absorbed before the 11d sync (informational: the sync delta window is `65b91fa190..f50b30de29`) |
| merged | the working tree | HEAD of `feature/masterwrought` (read from disk; `--merged-root` points the scan at a scratch copy) |

theirs predates every release/v0.40.0 commit after base (its merge base with the release
branch IS base, the #3493 v0.39.0 merge), so a name the release branch retired between base
and ours shows up on theirs only; the merge took ours' deletion by ordinary three-way
logic. Such names still need a row here (the parent set is the union, not base), and the
census annotates every MISSING and deleted name with its per-parent file sets, a
`base:yes/no` flag, and a packet-or-release attribution so the row can be written
mechanically.

## How Phase 17 re-runs it

    node scripts/merge_audit/symbol_census.mjs

Exit code nonzero when MISSING is non-empty, when an EXTRA has no explanation, when a
parent set is under its floor, or when a row in this file is defective. Options:
`--merged-root <dir>` (scan a scratch copy), `--json <out>` (the full sets),
`--release <ref>` (override the release parent, default `f50b30de29`; every later merge
commit on the branch's first-parent chain contributes its second parent automatically),
`--sync <ref>` (add a release tip that was synced without a merge commit),
`--no-auto-sync`, `--no-base`, `--limit <n>`. A MISSING name that no release parent
carries while base does is reported in its own release-attributable sub-list (the
release retired it and a packet parent was behind); it still requires a row here.

The mutation guard (run it whenever the script or an extractor changes): copy the five
roots into a scratch directory, delete ONE known name (un-export a function, drop one
`id:` line, drop one catalog leaf, drop one emit's `type:` literal), run with
`--merged-root <scratch>`, and confirm MISSING names exactly that symbol. A census nobody
has seen fail is not a census. The 11d pre-run's four outcomes are recorded in the Phase
11d ledger (`state.md`).

## The rules for a row

- Every row names the PHASE, the RULING (a ruling id, a commit, or a ledger section),
  the OLD name, the NEW name where it is a rename, the CLASS, and the REASON. A row that
  says only "deleted" is a defect and fails the census (`parseDeletionList`).
- Class is one of `export`, `content id`, `i18n key`, `simevent union`, `simevent emit`
  (consumed by the census, matched by the bare old name) or `literal-only` (a record for
  the reader: a field, a non-exported in-place copy, an aura id template, an overlay row,
  a test literal; nothing for the census to match).
- A name that survives under the same name in a different file is NOT missing; such
  relocations are recorded as `literal-only` rows so the history of the
  extraction-beats-in-place rule is in one place.
- Remove a row only when the name comes back on purpose; the census prints
  `WARN deletion-list rows not matching a missing name` when a row goes stale.
- Explained-extras rows (the `## Explained extras` tables, columns Class | Name |
  Phase | Ruling | Reason, with an OPTIONAL Path column between Name and Phase)
  follow the same discipline: every row names the PHASE, the RULING (a ruling id, a
  commit, or a ledger section), and a real REASON, and the Class is always a census
  class. The census consumes them in union with the script's `EXPLAINED_EXTRAS`
  constant; an EXTRA with no row in either place fails.
- RETIREMENT, NOT DELETION, for any id this branch has already pinned (added
  2026-09-01, ruling qr-19-shipped-id-golden-remint-cadence). Every branch-only
  item id the packet owns is already in `tests/shipped_item_ids.golden.json`, so
  the escape at `src/sim/content/CLAUDE.md` ("only an item that never left your
  unmerged feature branch may be deleted outright") is closed in practice: a
  Phase-20 cut of one of those ids is a RETIREMENT (keep the def, drop its
  acquisition paths, exemplar `RETIRED_HEROIC_ITEMS` in
  `src/sim/content/heroic_loot.ts`) plus a row in this file, never an outright
  delete and never a removal from the golden. A REMOVED golden line is that
  file's own stated signature of a dead shipped id.
- THE PATH COLUMN (added 2026-09-01, ruling qr-19-census-allowlist-path-asymmetry).
  A name that is real, exported and live but sits under one of the census's
  `EXCLUDED_PATH_PREFIXES` (`scripts/merge_audit/`, the resolved-i18n artifacts) can
  never be matched by any census class, so an extras row for it used to become an
  entry nothing could satisfy and was reported as "GONE from merged", the exact
  opposite of the truth. File such a name HERE with its Path and the census reports
  it as informational. Absent, the column reads empty and every older five-column
  table parses byte-unchanged. THE CHECK IS POSITIVE, not "is this outside census
  scope": the cell must name a file that actually sits under one of those prefixes
  AND carries a source extension, so a malformed cell (`file.ts:42`, `n/a`, a
  prose aside, a bare directory) is a DEFECT rather than an exemption. The
  negative form would have handed the informational verdict to almost any string,
  which is how a name that really is GONE from merged could buy itself one. The
  column is spelled `Path` and nothing else; and two rows for the same class and
  name that DISAGREE about Path are a defect, because the last row wins and would
  otherwise pick the verdict silently.

Phase 11e's crop roster adds forty rows below. A new content id is EXTRA in
THREE classes at once and in three different name forms: the bare id in
`contentIds`, the `file:id` key in `contentIdRows` (twice per crop, because its
`FARM_CROPS` row and its `items.ts` produce row share an id but not a file), and
the bare id again in `i18nKeys` for its English catalog name. The cost was
MEASURED at STEP 0 with a throwaway id before any authoring, which is what made
the budget a fact rather than a surprise; the i18n third of it was the part that

Names that came in through a release sync after the merge are not extras and are not
listed here: the synced release tip is a PARENT of the census (see the refs table), so
its names are simply present on a parent.

## release/v0.40.0 sync (PR #3549), the Phase 11d STEP 0 sync window

Deletions and renames in the window `65b91fa190..f50b30de29`: NONE, verified by a
per-class set diff of the two release tips through the census extractors (2026-08-21):
exports 17212 to 17221 with 0 deleted, content ids 2810 to 2810, i18n keys 17861 to
17866 with 0 deleted, SimEvent union 152 to 152, SimEvent emits 142 to 142. There is
nothing to record under this heading; a later sync that DOES delete or rename a name
adds its rows here with the reason read from the release diff
(`git diff <prior tip> <new tip> -- <file>`), never a bare "deleted".

What the window added (informational, all present on the release parent): the reliquary
HUD tracker family, 9 exports (`installTrackerStackAnchor`, `TrackerStackAnchor`,
`TrackerStackAnchorDeps`, `TrackerStackAnchorMeasure`, `InstalledTrackerStackAnchor`,
`trackerStackAnchorTopPx`, `TRACKER_STACK_ANCHOR_GAP_PX`, `ReliquaryTrackerWorld`,
`makeReliquaryTrackerInput`) and 5 i18n keys (`options.showReliquaryTracker`,
`reliquary.trackerToggleLabel`, `reliquary.trackerToggleShowHint`,
`reliquary.trackerToggleHideHint`, `settingsPage.ifShowReliquaryTracker`).

## release/v0.40.0 sync (PR #3506), the Phase 11d QA STEP 0 sync window

Second sync window, `f50b30de29..35a6481825`, merged at `5eade5c02e`. Deletions and
renames: NONE, verified the same way (2026-08-21): the window touches no file under
`src/sim/`, `server/`, `src/sim/content/` or `src/ui/i18n.catalog/`, so content ids,
i18n keys and both SimEvent classes are unchanged, and exports go 17221 to 17234 with
0 deleted. There is nothing to record under this heading either.

What the window added (informational, all present on the newer release parent): the
stale-focus Space fix, 13 exports across three new modules
(`src/game/stale_chrome_focus.ts`: `FocusedChromeEl`, `isStaleChromeButton`;
`src/ui/chrome_focus_wiring.ts`: `CHROME_GUARDED_PANELS`, `CHROME_TRACKER_BLURS`,
`wireChromeFocus`; `src/ui/pointer_blur.ts`: `ClickLike`, `BlurrableEl`,
`POINTER_FOCUS_PARK_SELECTOR`, `dropPointerFocus`, `blurIfPointerClick`,
`ListenerHost`, `bindPointerBlur`, `bindChromeButtonKeyGuard`). No i18n key, no
content id, no SimEvent.

The census picked `35a6481825` up automatically as a second release parent, off the
first-parent merge chain, with no flag: the refs table's promise that later syncs
contribute their second parents is now exercised rather than only stated.


## release/v0.41.0 sync (the seventeenth), the Phase 11m STEP 0 sync window

The census run at the merge 9f130d3b7c with `--ours 08607e5472 --theirs ff2837da1f --release ff2837da1f`. The parent union still includes the older synced release tips (auto-derived off the first-parent chain), so a name the NEWEST release retired reads as packet-attributable there; every such name is attributed here to its release commit instead. Rows written mechanically from the census JSON (scratchpad gen_deletion_rows.mjs at the sync). Two things to read the rows with. First, the `(base, ours, theirs)` triple in every Reason cell is the census's own presence triple, and its `base` is the script's `BASE_REF` e56707a675 (the refs table above), NOT this sync's merge base 9a89e3483e; the two differ wherever the branch changed a name between those commits (`inventoryCount` reads base:no because e56707a675 carried it unexported, while 9a89e3483e carries the export). Second, attribution, over the section's 487 rows: 449 rows name the release commit that dropped the name inside `9a89e3483e..ff2837da1f`; 10 rows name the branch commit that dropped or renamed it inside `9a89e3483e..08607e5472` (nine `src/ui/i18n.catalog/guide.ts` key renames, each Reason naming the new key, plus the `inventoryCount` export); 8 rows carry no commit because neither current parent holds the name (only an older synced release tip in the census union does: the release retired it between syncs and the branch had already absorbed that deletion); and 20 `content row` rows carry no commit of their own because the row name (`file:id`) is a census construct no commit text contains, so `git log -S` on it matches nothing. Those 20 are attributed through their content-id twins and by `git log -S<id>` on the file path, both verified at the merge audit's second fix round: 1c74387b4c (the Sowfield demolition) for the eighteen `src/sim/content/vale_cup.ts` rows and `src/sim/content/zone1.ts:groundskeeper_bram`, and 912cd0b531 for `src/sim/content/graveyards.ts:gy_vale_chapel`. Every commit a row names was re-checked at that round to sit inside its window and to be listed by `git log -S<name>` on the file that carried it (the probe for an i18n key is its last segment, since the catalog nests keys): a `-S` run WITHOUT the path returns the newest commit whose substring count changed, which is how nine rows first carried the wrong commit.

## Phase 17 reconciliation deletions (2026-08-30)

Rows written at the Phase 17 delivery-gate re-run, discharging the MISSING set the
run found at tip b88cf76f80 (exports 4, i18n keys 2, simevent emits 2). Three
clusters. FIRST, the Phase 14 QA calendar-cache deletion: branch commit eca196811d
deliberately deleted the four production-dead per-key calendar caches from
src/game/utc_day.ts (its body: "The four production-dead per-key calendar caches are
deleted (the single-instant feed is the only path)"); three of the four sat on census
parents (the fourth, currentResetRemainingSec, was packet-authored after the extras
discipline lapsed and died unrecorded). The replacement surface survives at tip: the
pure `*Of(at)` primitives (`resetDayOf`, `eventLeadDayOf`, `nextResetMsOf`,
`resetRemainingSecOf`) plus the `feedSimCalendar` single-instant feed. SECOND, two
names the RELEASE itself retired between synced tips: the census's
release-attribution annotation fires only when BASE carries the name, and these were
born upstream after base and retired upstream too, so the run misfiles them as
packet-attributable; each row names the retiring release commit, found by
`git log -S` over the release span and verified in its diff (the 2026-08-25
section's shape). THIRD, the two zone-celebration SimEvents whose emit sites Phase
13 made helper-shaped: branch commit 5bbfcb0450 extracted the shared
`announceZoneCelebration` prologue (rule of three: `masterworkZone`, `attunedZone`,
`legendaryForgedZone`), so each event literal now sits in a caller's builder arrow
one indirection PAST the named `emitToZonePlayers` fanout, where the emits extractor
cannot see it. The events are still declared (src/sim/types.ts), still handled
(src/ui/hud.ts), and still fire (tests/professions_attunement_online.test.ts,
tests/masterwork_zone_broadcast.test.ts, tests/orange_promotion.test.ts). Their
class is `simevent emit` rather than `literal-only` DELIBERATELY: the rules above
say `literal-only` is "nothing for the census to match", a record only, and the
census genuinely lost these names from the emits class, so it needs a consumable row
to discharge them; the blind spot itself is additionally pinned by name in the
script's `SIM_EVENT_UNION_ONLY`.

| Class | Old name | New name | Phase | Ruling | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| export | `currentResetDay` | | 14 QA | branch commit eca196811d (fix(ui): follow a bagged Perfecting selection through a bag shift by ordinal); the Phase 14 QA ledger | the per-key daily-reset-window cache, one of the four production-dead per-key calendar caches the commit body deletes ("the single-instant feed is the only path"); the pure `resetDayOf(at)` and `feedSimCalendar` survive (base:yes, ours:yes, theirs:yes; src/game/utc_day.ts) |

| export | `currentEventLeadDay` | | 14 QA | branch commit eca196811d (fix(ui): follow a bagged Perfecting selection through a bag shift by ordinal); the Phase 14 QA ledger | the per-key early-open probe cache of the same four; the pure `eventLeadDayOf(at)` survives; present on release parents only (born upstream after base, absorbed by sync, then deleted by the branch commit), hence base:no, ours:no, theirs:no (src/game/utc_day.ts) |

## Phase 18 STEP 0 deletions (2026-08-30, the eighth v0.41.0 sync)

Rows written at the census re-run on the merge commit 4f72218ed4 (release tip
3e801dc925 into feature/masterwrought 4f9b785794), discharging the MISSING set it
found (exports 2). Both names are the SAME class the Phase 17 reconciliation's
second cluster records: born upstream after base and retired upstream too, so the
base-gated release-attribution annotation cannot fire and the run misfiles them as
packet-attributable. The release's raid consolidation absorbed the arena-wall
camera-occluder fade module `src/render/arena_wall_fade.ts` (the gated sightline fade
of release commit 67eceab7d1, itself synced at the seventh sync e19d832b47) into
`src/render/dungeon_wall_occlusion.ts`, which now drives both occlusion modes; the
deleted module's pins moved with it to `tests/occluder_fade_gate.test.ts`, and the
merged `tests/monolith_budget.test.ts` dungeon.ts row records the same absorption in
the release's own words. Found by `git log -S` over the release span and verified in
the deleting commit's diff.

## Phase 18 perfection sweep deletions (2026-08-31, unit U-SFX-UI)

Branch-authored deletions, not a sync window: names that sit on census parents and
are gone from merged, so the run reports them MISSING until a row discharges each.
All four are exports of `src/ui/map_window_view.ts` removed by one item,
map-marker-dead-exports (the actionable half of bf-residuals-batch, whose ledger
entry pre-sanctions deleting the twins TOGETHER). The two hit-testers were
production-dead: `tests/map_window_view.test.ts` was their only consumer, while every
live hit-test goes through `mapPointMarkerHitsInto` (whose station and farm arms keep
their own ordering, distance and radius-miss coverage in that same suite, so the
deletion drops no real coverage). The two radius constants went with them because the
pair were their only readers: the live path takes ONE shared radius argument, and
`MAP_NAVIGATION_HIT_RADIUS` is the only per-family constant it consults.

| export | `farmPatchMarkerAt` | | 18 | qr-18-REOPEN, items map-marker-dead-exports and bf-residuals-batch | its farm-patch twin, deleted in the same change per the ledger's delete-both-together note; the live farm hit-test is the `'farm'` arm of `mapPointMarkerHitsInto` |
| export | `MAP_STATION_HIT_RADIUS` | | 18 | qr-18-REOPEN, item map-marker-dead-exports | the station badge's own hit radius, read only by `stationMarkerAt` and by that function's now-deleted test arm |

## Phase 18 perfection sweep deletions (2026-08-31, unit U-I18N)

Branch-authored deletions, not a sync window: names that sit on census parents and are
gone from merged, so the run reports them MISSING until a row discharges each. 423
names, from two items, and ONE ROW PER NAME because that is the only form the census
accepts: `parseDeletionList` keys an exact-string `Map` on the old name
(`deletionByClass[row.cls].set(row.oldName, row)`), with no wildcard, count or pattern
field, so a grouped row would match nothing AND become a stale row itself. The mechanism
is therefore written once, here, rather than 423 times.

**The 420 `<locale>.abilityUi.*` names (item abilities-inert-locale-arms).** Ten locales
x 42 leaf paths, every one a leaf of the inline non-English arms of `abilityStrings` in
`src/ui/i18n.catalog/abilities.ts`, which the file itself documented as legacy
(pre-i18n.locales split) and unused at runtime. **The recorded premise for the strip was
WRONG in a way a future reader must not repeat: these arms are NOT unread.** They are read,
by `merge.ts` (`abilityStrings.<locale>.abilityUi`) and `items.ts`
(`classAbilityNames.<locale>.entities`) - whose OWN non-English arms are the dead root,
since `index.ts` takes `.en` from every one of those objects. That is why 30 dependent
spread lines in those two siblings went in the same change, and why the strip took
`abilities.ts` from 9177 lines to 2131.

Inertness was therefore proved empirically rather than asserted: the resolved
`abilityUi` + `entities.abilities` surface was rebuilt from HEAD's catalog and from the
stripped one and compared value by value, giving **12,194 identical values across 13
locales, zero moved** (the ten locales with inline arms, plus the `es_ES`/`fr_CA` dialect
copies and `en`). No player-visible string changed. Do NOT restore these keys: the
translations that actually ship live in `src/ui/i18n.locales/<lang>.ts`, these copies had
drifted through several rename waves, and restoring them would put roughly 143 stale
ability renderings per non-Latin locale back into the tree where nothing reads them.

**The 3 `sim.rift.detonate*` names (item dead-detonate-sibling-keys).** Retired keys, not
inert copies: nothing emits them. Row detail below.

## Explained extras (2026-08-30, the Phase 17 reconciliation: phases 11e to 16)

The explained-extras discipline stopped after 11e: phases 11f through 16 kept
authoring exports, content rows, i18n keys, and SimEvents that exist on no census
parent, and nobody wrote the rows. This section closes that lapse mechanically at
the Phase 17 delivery gate. Every row was attributed by finding the INTRODUCING
branch commit (`git log --reverse -S<name> -- <the files the census reports>`,
candidates verified by re-running the census extractors over the file blob at the
candidate and at its first parent: absent before, present after), then mapping
that commit to its phase by first-parent position against the closing docs-stamp
commits of docs/prd/masterwrought/progress.md, cross-checked against the phase's
Started/Completed dates. A name whose introducing commit is a merge commit, or
that cannot be attributed, gets NO row here (that is the merge-artifact class the
extras check exists to catch) and is reported to the operator instead.

## Explained extras (2026-09-01, the Phase 19B UI wave)

Names authored by the Phase 19B units; per the standing rule every unit that
authors a new name appends its rows in the same change.

| Class | Name | Phase | Ruling | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| exports | `FARM_FINE_PRODUCE_ITEM_IDS` | 19B | qr-19-farm-fine-produce-kind-label | the twelve farm fine twins derived off the crop catalog, the membership the tooltip kind line reads so a farm twin prints "Fine Material" beside the nine ore, log and herb fine grades; deliberately a SEPARATE set from FARM_MATERIAL_ITEM_IDS (which also carries the seeds, the base produce, the husks and the two knob supplies, so an arm over it would label a seed) and deliberately NOT a MATERIAL_GRADES widening, since materialGradeIds walks that table alone and a twin inside it would begin satisfying a recipe asking for base produce (src/sim/content/farm_crops.ts) |
| exports | `closeReportWindow` | 19B | qr-19-report-window-focus-trap-carveout | the report window's one close path, so the two [data-close] handlers, the submit success and Hud.closeManagedWindow all release the shared focus trap and return focus to the opener instead of the bare hide the window used to fall to; the export is what lets the coordinator's `case 'report-window':` arm reach it without the module importing Hud (src/ui/report_window.ts) |
| exports | `runFencedCharacterUpdate` | 19C | qr-19-live-nonce-fence-write-loss | the nonce-fenced character UPDATE run with the characters row lock (SELECT 1 ... FOR NO KEY UPDATE) taken FIRST; extracted beside the statement builder so the three DIRECT live save paths each swap one call line and server/db.ts pays nothing for the lock fix (the escrow caller saveCharacterStateOnClient is excluded, escrow occupancy invariant) (server/character_save_statement.ts) |
| exports | `CHARACTER_SAVE_ROW_LOCK_SQL` | 19C | qr-19-live-nonce-fence-write-loss | the SELECT 1 FROM characters WHERE id = $1 AND realm = $2 FOR NO KEY UPDATE the live fenced UPDATE takes first, so the uncorrelated fence InitPlan is evaluated with the row already held and a mid-wait lease displacement is not invisible; FOR NO KEY UPDATE not the offline arm's FOR UPDATE, to avoid FK-child contention on the hot path (server/character_save_statement.ts) |
| exports | `liveSaveFence` | 19C | qr-19-live-nonce-fence-write-loss | the live-session fence-shape picker, moved beside the statement builder (the process holder passed in as an argument to avoid a db.ts import cycle) to pay for the row-lock fix's growth (server/character_save_statement.ts) |
| exports | `cleanMetadataText` | 19C | qr-19-sold-volume-four-seam-wiring | the request-metadata trim-and-cap helper, extracted from server/db.ts so the market sold-volume schema wiring lands without growing the db.ts monolith (server/clean_metadata_text.ts) |
| exports | `WOC_MARKET_SOLD_VOLUME_TAIL` | 19C | qr-19-sold-volume-four-seam-wiring | the /metrics gauge name for the sold-volume write FIFO occupancy (measure=depth/coalesced), added in D147's review round so the live per-sale write path is observable, mirroring woc_bank_ledger_tail (server/http/game_metrics.ts) |
| exports | `WOC_MARKET_SOLD_VOLUME_TAIL_DROPPED_SALES_TOTAL` | 19C | qr-19-sold-volume-four-seam-wiring | the /metrics counter name for sales dropped at the sold-volume FIFO admission cap (server/http/game_metrics.ts) |
| exports | `MARKET_SOLD_VOLUME_SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS` | 19C | qr-19-sold-volume-four-seam-wiring | the bounded deadline the shutdown drain gives the sold-volume FIFO before the lease sweep, added in D147's review round so a wedged database cannot skip releaseAllCharacterLeases (server/market_sold_volume.ts) |

RELOCATED 2026-09-01 by ruling qr-19-census-allowlist-path-asymmetry (masterwrought
Phase 19D, D130): the five `scripts/merge_audit/golden_composition` names
(`releaseParentRefs`, `newestReleaseRef`, `classifyLineage`, `isAnchorPath`,
`Lineage`) were recorded here because an extras row naming them became an
allowlist entry nothing could satisfy, which the census then reported as "GONE
from merged". That is fixed at the tool: an extras row may now declare a Path,
and a row under one of the census's excluded prefixes is reported as
INFORMATIONAL instead. The five moved back into the "Explained extras" table
below, where a reader looks for them, and their presence there is what keeps
the new arm covered end to end rather than by fixture alone.

## Explained extras (2026-09-01, the Phase 19D tooling wave; Path column)

The Path column is OPTIONAL and new (ruling qr-19-census-allowlist-path-asymmetry).
It names the file a symbol lives in when that file sits under one of the census's
own `EXCLUDED_PATH_PREFIXES`, so the tool can report the row as informational
rather than as an entry nothing can satisfy. A Path inside census scope is a
DEFECT, not an exemption: `parseExplainedExtras` checks the claim.

| Class | Name | Path | Phase | Ruling | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| exports | `pruneMissingRows` |  | 19D | qr-19-stale-client-deploy-window | the pure, injectable half of scripts/ci_shard_weights_harvest.mjs's new --prune-missing mode: drops every weight row whose file is gone and MOVES THE ATTRIBUTION with it (a harvested row decrements __provenance.harvestedFiles, a carried row is paid by deleting its carried entry), so the table keeps its harvestedFiles-plus-carried-equals-rows contract. Extracted rather than left in the CLI branch because that arithmetic is the only thing that makes the write safe and it was untestable inside the entry. It exists because D172 retires tests/stale_client_rollout.test.ts, tests/ci_shard_partition.test.ts reds on a weight row naming an absent file, and the full harvest needs a green FULL-MODE CI run no local-only wave can produce (scripts/lib/ci_shard_weight_carry.mjs) |
| exports | `PRUNE_MAX_DROPS` |  | 19D | qr-19-stale-client-deploy-window | the default bound on how many rows a single --prune-missing may drop, added at the review round. carriedDefects cannot catch a mass prune by construction (pruneMissingRows preserves harvestedFiles-plus-carried-equals-rows whatever it removes, so an EMPTIED table is self-consistent and raises no defect), and a wrong-root or wrong-tree exists callback is the live trigger: a sparse checkout, a partial clone, a branch predating most tests. Exported so the pin can assert the shipped value rather than restate it (scripts/lib/ci_shard_weight_carry.mjs) |
| exports | `paintRestIndicator` |  | 19D | qr-19-hud-coordinator-fanout-exemption | the rest badge's DOM half, added at the review round. #pf-rest carries BOTH data-i18n-title and data-i18n-aria pointing at the bare Resting key, so the static shell pass stamps that over both on every language change, and the live code corrected only the TITLE: a screen reader announced Resting for the whole of a meal while the tooltip read Eating. Writing both sinks from ONE resolved string is the fix, and giving them one function is what stops the next reader correcting only one of them again. Facet-routed and raw-write-free (src/ui/rest_indicator_painter.ts) |
| exports | `COMPASS_ROSE_IDS` |  | 19D | qr-19-hud-coordinator-fanout-exemption | the rose ids in strip order, DERIVED from the ROSE table in the same file rather than restated, so a ninth point cannot be added to CardinalId and leave the DOM pool short. Lives with the pure derivation, not with the painter, which is what makes the derivation possible (src/ui/compass.ts) |
| exports | `CompassMarkElements` |  | 19D | qr-19-hud-coordinator-fanout-exemption | the strip's element pool type, keyed by the language-agnostic rose id (CardinalId), which is what lets the relabel resolve t() from the key rather than from stored text (src/ui/compass_strip_painter.ts) |
| exports | `buildCompassMarks` |  | 19D | qr-19-hud-coordinator-fanout-exemption | builds the eight rose-label spans once and returns the pool; it localizes them, which is precisely why relabelCompassMarks has to exist, since nothing else ever writes them again (src/ui/compass_strip_painter.ts) |
| exports | `relabelCompassMarks` |  | 19D | qr-19-hud-coordinator-fanout-exemption | rewrites every rose label with fresh t(). THE REASON THE MODULE EXISTS: the labels were written once at pool build, so a runtime language switch left the whole strip in the previous locale forever, one of the seven defects the hud.ts memo audit found (src/ui/compass_strip_painter.ts) |
| exports | `paintCompassMarks` |  | 19D | qr-19-hud-coordinator-fanout-exemption | slides the visible marks and hides the rest, taking the caller's reused Set so the per-frame path still allocates nothing; extracted with its pool so creator and painter cannot drift apart (src/ui/compass_strip_painter.ts) |
